Let me look at how DDL is structured so migrateV3 can reuse. The DDL array includes all tables with IF NOT EXISTS. migrateV2 re-runs whole DDL. For v3 I'll write a dedicated rebuild.

Let me write:

```ts
const PROBE_V2_COLUMNS = 'id, session_id, claim_id, interviewer_id, round, question, rationale, parent_probe_id, quote, start, end, exact, answer_text, answer_mode, verdict, created_at';

function migrateV3(db: Db): void {
  db.exec('PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF');
  db.exec('BEGIN');
  try {
    db.exec('DROP INDEX IF EXISTS probe_by_claim');
    db.exec('ALTER TABLE probe RENAME TO probe_v2');
    for (const statement of DDL) db.exec(statement);
    db.exec('INSERT INTO probe (' + PROBE_V2_COLUMNS + ') SELECT ' + PROBE_V2_COLUMNS + ' FROM probe_v2');
    db.exec('DROP TABLE probe_v2');
    db.exec('PRAGMA user_version = 3');
    db.exec('COMMIT');
  } catch (err) {
    db.exec('ROLLBACK');
    throw err;
  } finally {
    db.exec('PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON');
  }
}
```

Careful: DDL contains claim with IF NOT EXISTS — re-running is harmless since claim exists.

Wait, but `for (const statement of DDL) db.exec(statement)` after renaming probe: the probe CREATE will create fresh table without unique. Good.

Also the index probe_by_claim gets recreated by DDL. Good.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I found a real bug: probe table has UNIQUE (session_id, claim_id, round, interviewer_id), but MAX_PROBES_PER_TURN = 2 means one interviewer can ask 2 questions on the same claim in the same round → UNIQUE constraint violation on persist. This is a P0 bug: it would crash real runs. Wait — did live runs work? In live runs, probes were 12 over 3 rounds with 8 claims; maybe no interviewer asked twice on same claim same round. But the scripted demo: DEMO plans had 2 questions per claim by... let me think. In the scripted demo, R1 tech picks C1, C2 (one question each), R2 picks second questions. So same claim same round never had 2 probes from one interviewer. But it CAN happen: MAX_PROBES_PER_TURN=2 and canProbe allows maxProbesPerInterviewerPerClaim=2 in the same round. So a model that asks 2 questions about C1 in round 1 passes all gates (quota 2) and then persist crashes with UNIQUE constraint → the whole run fails with 500 after doing all the work.

That's a genuine P0 bug found by code review. Need to fix: either drop the UNIQUE constraint (migrate to v3) or change the gate to limit 1 per (claim, round, interviewer). Which is right?

Think: the semantic intent of the constraint was "one probe per interviewer per claim per round" — a round is one interviewer call, so per call per claim, asking 2 questions is allowed by MAX_PROBES_PER_TURN. So the constraint is wrong. Best fix: schema v3 removing that UNIQUE constraint (keep an index for lookup). Also `notesFor` says "你还能问 N 次" per claim.

Alternatively change probe.ts to allow only 1 probe per (claim, interviewer) per round — but that changes MAX_PROBES_PER_TURN semantics and reduces depth. Hmm, but wait: parentProbeId chains same (claimId, interviewerId) — chain is across rounds. If two questions in the same round on the same claim, they'd both chain to the previous round's probe, and the second one's parent would be the first (lastProbeId updated). That's fine.

So the right fix is schema v3: remove the UNIQUE (session_id, claim_id, round, interviewer_id) constraint, keep index. That requires a table rebuild migration.

Let me verify with a test: this is a real bug I should fix, and it's exactly "整体核一遍代码" output.

Design decision: which is correct — the schema or the gate? I'll argue: MAX_PROBES_PER_TURN=2 exists so one interviewer can dig into 2 different claims, but asking twice about the same claim in one turn is legitimate (a follow-up inside the same turn). The unique constraint was written when I assumed one probe per claim per interviewer per round. The ledger already tracks rounds per claim; enforcing one-per-round-per-claim-per-interviewer would silently cap depth at 3 total instead of 6. So relax the schema.

Wait, but rounds per claim: rounds counts every probe on the claim (any interviewer). So maxRoundsPerClaim=3 means a claim gets at most 3 probes total across interviewers. Hmm, with 3 interviewers × 2 per claim each = 6 potential but rounds caps at 3. So asking 2 in one round eats 2 of 3 rounds. That's consistent.

So fixing schema to v3 is right.

Let me implement:
- SCHEMA_VERSION = 3
- DDL probe: remove UNIQUE line, keep index probe_by_claim. Maybe add index on (session_id, claim_id, round, interviewer_id)? The existing index covers lookups.
- migrateV3: rebuild probe table (rename to probe_v2, create new from DDL, insert select, drop).

Note migrateV2 currently runs DDL (which after my change creates v3 probe table). If a v1 DB upgrades: current=1 → run MIGRATIONS[2] (migrateV2) which uses DDL to create new tables — but DDL now is v3 shape, so it would create the probe table already without UNIQUE, then MIGRATIONS[3] would rebuild again (harmless but wasteful). Actually migrateV2 sets PRAGMA user_version = 2 then loop continues to 3 → migrateV3 rebuilds. It works but does double work. Cleaner: make migrateV2 use a v2-specific DDL. Hmm. Let me make migrateV2 set user_version=2 and let migrateV3 run; migrateV3 renames probe → probe_v2, creates fresh via DDL, copies. Since the DDL created table has no UNIQUE, copying works. It's correct, just one extra rebuild for very old DBs. Acceptable, and I'll note it. Actually simpler: keep DDL as the single source of truth for the current shape, and migrations just re-shape. Fine.

Also the INSERT for migrateV3 needs column list: same as PROBE_V1_COLUMNS.

Now I have a clear picture of the confirmed bug: probe unique constraint `UNIQUE (session_id, claim_id, round, interviewer_id)` collides when the same interviewer asks 2 questions about the same claim in the same round (MAX_PROBES_PER_TURN = 2). This actually happens in real runs? In the earlier live run: 10 claims, 12 probes. Possibly it never happened because each interviewer spread across claims. But it's a real latent bug — probe.ts explicitly allows up to maxPerTurn=2 per interviewer, and same claim can be asked twice by same interviewer in a round.

Wait — but canProbe has perInterviewer limit 2 per claim total, so one interviewer can ask 2 questions on the same claim. If both in the same round → UNIQUE violation → persistResult throws inside transaction → whole run fails at the very end (after all model calls). That's a P0 data-loss bug: a full run is thrown away.

Let me fix: remove that UNIQUE constraint (schema v3 migration), since the natural key should be (session_id, id) only. Actually the unique constraint was intended as a guard. But probe ids are P<seq> unique within session. The constraint adds no value and breaks a legal case.

Plan:
1. Bump SCHEMA_VERSION to 3, add migrateV3 that rebuilds probe table without the UNIQUE (session_id, claim_id, round, interviewer_id) — actually simpler: recreate probe table with UNIQUE replaced by a plain index.

Actually simpler: make v3 migration just drop the unique index... but it's a table constraint, not a named index in SQLite; UNIQUE in CREATE TABLE creates an auto index (sqlite_autoindex_probe_N) which cannot be dropped. So table rebuild required.

Let me write it.

migrateV3: rebuild probe table:
```
function migrateV3(db: Db): void {
  db.exec('PRAGMA foreign_keys = OFF');
  db.exec('BEGIN');
  try {
    db.exec('DROP INDEX IF EXISTS probe_by_claim');
    db.exec('ALTER TABLE probe RENAME TO probe_v2');
    db.exec(PROBE_TABLE_V3);   // the CREATE TABLE statement for probe (v3)
    db.exec('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS probe_by_claim ON probe(session_id, claim_id, round)');
    db.exec('INSERT INTO probe (' + PROBE_V1_COLUMNS + ') SELECT ' + PROBE_V1_COLUMNS + ' FROM probe_v2');
    db.exec('DROP TABLE probe_v2');
    db.exec('PRAGMA user_version = 3');
    db.exec('COMMIT');
  } ...
}
```
Note PROBE_V1_COLUMNS is the same column list as v2/v3 (columns unchanged). Rename it to PROBE_COLUMNS for clarity? It's used in migrateV2 too. I'll keep name but maybe rename to PROBE_COLUMNS. Let's rename to PROBE_COLUMNS since columns never changed across versions. Careful: grep usages.

Also, ALTER TABLE probe RENAME TO probe_v2 — with legacy_alter_table off, SQLite will rewrite references in other tables' FK clauses pointing to probe. No other table references probe. Fine.

But careful: when renaming a table, SQLite also renames its indexes? Indexes attached to the renamed table follow it (probe_by_claim becomes index of probe_v2). We drop the index before renaming, so fine.

Also the FK from probe to claim: after rename, probe_v2 has FK to claim. Fine.

Let me restructure using arrays:

```
const PROBE_INDEX_SQL = 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS probe_by_claim ON probe(session_id, claim_id, round)';

/** probe 表的建表语句。v2 带「一轮一条」唯一约束，v3 去掉了它（见文件头第 4 点）。 */
function probeTableSql(turnUnique: boolean): string {
  const lines = [
    'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS probe (',
    '  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES session(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,',
    ...
    '  PRIMARY KEY (session_id, id),',
  ];
  if (turnUnique) lines.push('  UNIQUE (session_id, claim_id, round, interviewer_id),');
  lines.push('  FOREIGN KEY (session_id, claim_id) REFERENCES claim(session_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE');
  lines.push(')');
  return lines.join('\n');
}
```
Using String.fromCharCode(10)? In the file I can write `join(String.fromCharCode(10))` — no, I can write `'\n'` literally in the file content (it's a TS source file; `\n` inside a normal single-quoted string is fine and my String.raw wrapper preserves the backslash-n as two chars). Yes! String.raw preserves `\n` as backslash+n which TS then interprets as newline. 

Now full DDL list function:

```
function schemaStatements(opts: { probeTurnUnique: boolean }): readonly string[] {
  return [
    SESSION_TABLE,
    CLAIM_TABLE,
    CLAIM_INDEX,
    probeTableSql(opts.probeTurnUnique),
    PROBE_INDEX_SQL,
    EVENT_TABLE,
    LLM_CALL_TABLE,
    LLM_CALL_INDEX_1,
    LLM_CALL_INDEX_2,
  ];
}

export const DDL: readonly string[] = schemaStatements({ probeTurnUnique: false });
/** v2 当时的建表语句，迁移步骤必须冻结在那个时点。 */
```

Now add a regression test in session-pipeline.test.ts: one interviewer asks two probes about the same claim in the same round, must persist. But RUN_OPTS uses maxProbesPerInterviewerPerClaim: 1 and maxRoundsPerClaim: 1, so a second probe about C1 would be blocked by the budget gate. So the regression test needs a separate run opts with higher budget, and a probe handler that returns 2 probes on the first call.

Let me write it: PROBE_TWO = JSON with two probes both claimId C1, different questions, both quoting QUOTE. Budget: maxRoundsPerClaim: 1, maxProbesPerInterviewerPerClaim: 2, maxTotalProbes: 4.

Then runAndPersist with panel ['tech'], and handler returns PROBE_TWO for 'probe' purpose on first call, and empty probes on subsequent calls (round 2 would be blocked since maxRoundsPerClaim=1 anyway → loop ends).

So calls: extract 1, probe 1, answer 2, verdict 1, suggest 0 (verdict solid → skipped) = 5. Events: stage,claims,stage,probes,stage,answers,budget,stage,verdicts,stage,suggestions = 11 (same structure).

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

PANEL_SIZE 是死配置。用户要求「整体过一遍流程，测试每一个功能，整体核一遍代码」。这个死配置应该处置：要么接线，要么删。接线成本低：在 serve.ts 里读 PANEL_SIZE，通过 AppOptions 的 defaultPanel 传进去，限制 1-6，超范围就用默认。但注意 app.ts 的 createApp 用的是 DEFAULT_PANEL 常量。

我看一下 app.ts 的 AppOptions 与两处 DEFAULT_PANEL 使用，以及 run 路由的 panelOf。设计：给 AppOptions 加 `defaultPanel?: readonly InterviewerRole[]`，`resolvePanel(opts)` 返回 opts.defaultPanel ?? DEFAULT_PANEL。`/api/contracts` 与 `/run` 都用它。

不过要小心：面板大小限制 1-6，但 ROLES 只有 5 个。PANEL_SIZE 语义是「并发面试官数量上限」。取前 N 个角色：DEFAULT_PANEL 是 ['tech','hr','hiring_manager']。若 PANEL_SIZE=4 → 取 tech,hr,hiring_manager,stress？这会改变默认演示行为，风险大——replay 卡带是按三位面试官录的，改了默认面板整个离线演示就崩。

更安全的做法：PANEL_SIZE 用于**限制请求里 panel 的长度上限**，即 `?panel=a,b,c,d,e,f` 超过 N 个就截断/报错。这样默认面板不变，配置真的有语义。这个语义与 .env.example 的注释「并发质询的面试官数量上限（1-6）」吻合。

实现：AppOptions 加 `maxPanelSize?: number`（默认 6，来自 PANEL_SIZE）。panelOf 需要访问它，所以改成 resolvePanel(raw, max)。当请求的 panel 超过上限时…… 报错还是截断？按 D15 的思路：解释性接口显式报错。但 /run 是宽容过滤。这里我选：超过上限时截断并保持宽容（/run 已是宽容），但 /api/contracts 也是解析同一个 panel，行为要一致。

嗯，其实更简单也更诚实：**超出上限直接 400**，文案「面试官数量最多 N 位（PANEL_SIZE）」。但这会让 /run 变得比以前严格，破坏「宽容过滤」决策 D15……不过 D15 说的是「未知角色」的宽容 vs 报错，不是数量上限。

我觉得最省事且无副作用的做法是：把 PANEL_SIZE 接成**上限**，超出时取前 N 个（截断），并在响应里如实体现（因为返回的 panel 字段就是实际生效的面板）。这样不引入新的错误分支，也不改默认行为。可是静默截断正好违反 D15 的教训……

让我重新想。用户的真实关切是「说明书里的配置必须真的生效」。最小、最诚实、零风险的接线：**PANEL_SIZE 决定 `/run` 不带 `?panel=` 时的默认面板**——不，会破坏卡带。

或者：干脆删掉这个死配置。理由充分：面板由请求参数决定，默认三位是产品决策（与卡带、截图、文档全部对齐），没有「并发数上限」这个独立概念——MAX_PROBES_PER_TURN 与预算已经限流。删掉它，.env.example 更诚实。

但「面试官数量上限」确实是个合理的安全阀：请求 ?panel= 可以传 5 个，就并发 5 路调用。给它加个上限确实有意义。那就做成：**上限 = PANEL_SIZE（默认 6，合法 1..5），超出时 400 并说明**。这跟 /api/contracts 对未知角色的显式报错一致（D15），而且 /run 的 panel 走同一个解析函数时也会 400——需要检查测试是否传了超限 panel。测试里最多传两位，不会触发。

我倾向这样做。先看 app.ts 相关代码。

</details>

Now I want to wire PANEL_SIZE properly: it should be the default panel size — a server-side default that limits the default panel when no `panel` query is given. Let's implement `defaultPanel()` in run.ts or app.ts:

Semantics: PANEL_SIZE limits how many interviewers in the default panel (DEFAULT_PANEL slice). Validate 1..6. Better: put in app.ts a function `panelFromEnv()` that reads PANEL_SIZE, clamps to [1, DEFAULT_PANEL.length], returns DEFAULT_PANEL.slice(0, n). Then `panelOf(raw) ?? defaultPanel`.

Actually a more meaningful feature: allow adding roles from the full ROLES list. DEFAULT_PANEL has 3 roles. PANEL_SIZE=4 means 4 interviewers. Hmm — a sensible implementation: when PANEL_SIZE > DEFAULT_PANEL.length, extend with roles from ROLES beyond DEFAULT_PANEL (stress, domain_expert). That matches the doc "并发质询的面试官数量上限（1-6）".

Let me implement in run.ts? Better in app.ts near ROLES. But also /api/contracts default panel uses DEFAULT_PANEL — should also respect env. Let's create a shared helper in app.ts:

```ts
const DEFAULT_PANEL_SIZE = 3;
const MIN_PANEL_SIZE = 1;
const MAX_PANEL_SIZE = ROLES.length; // 5

export function defaultPanel(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): readonly InterviewerRole[] {
  const raw = env['PANEL_SIZE'];
  const parsed = raw === undefined ? NaN : Number.parseInt(raw, 10);
  const size = Number.isFinite(parsed) ? clamp(parsed, MIN_PANEL_SIZE, MAX_PANEL_SIZE) : DEFAULT_PANEL_SIZE;
  const ordered = [...DEFAULT_PANEL, ...ROLES.filter(r => !DEFAULT_PANEL.includes(r))] as InterviewerRole[];
  return ordered.slice(0, size);
}
```

Hmm, DEFAULT_PANEL is 3; ROLES order is tech, hr, hiring_manager, stress, domain_expert. So ordered = tech,hr,hiring_manager,stress,domain_expert. size default 3 → same as today. PANEL_SIZE=4 → +stress. PANEL_SIZE=5 → +domain_expert.

If PANEL_SIZE env is set to 4, replay would fail (cassette-missing) — that's honest but the .env.example ships PANEL_SIZE=4, which would break the out-of-the-box demo if user copies .env.example → .env! That's a real risk: README says `cp .env.example .env`. If PANEL_SIZE=4 changes the default panel, then `npm run preview` with .env copied would try 4 interviewers and replay would fail with cassette-missing. That's bad.

- Set .env.example PANEL_SIZE=3 (matching default and the recorded cassettes) and document that changing it requires re-recording (live mode) — reasonable.
- Or don't wire it and delete it.

```text
404|1. **tools/repro/repro-check.mjs**（`npm run repro`）：把仓库当成评委第一次见到它的样子——

408|   「10 分钟极速复现」这条承诺有据可查；数字写进 README 与 tools/repro/README.md。
```

```text
133|| D7 | 锚点坐标以 quote 回原文重新定位为准，模型给的 start/end 只当提示 | 模型常常把坐标算错却把 quote 抄对，信任哪一边决定了锚定精度的下限 |
134|| D8 | 存储连接一建立就自动建表，只读连接除外 | 「忘记 migrate」是一种不会在进程内测试里暴露、只会在真实进程里炸的错；把默认路径设成正确的那条，比在每处调用点记得调用更可靠 |
135|| D9 | 提交门禁纳入真进程冒烟（npm run smoke） | 同进程的 app.request 测试全绿不等于服务能跑；进程边界上的坑（表没建、端口没监听、SSE 不结束）只有真起进程才看得见 |
136|| D10 | 离线演示卡带用脚本合成，且卡带自曝身份（model=scripted-demo） | 评委没有 Key 也要能看到完整流程；合成数据可反复重跑、逐字节可比，但绝不允许冒充真实模型输出；见 ADR-0002 |
137|| D11 | 前端不把 @crossexam/shared 引进包，只走 domain.ts 子路径复用标签 | shared 经 util.ts 依赖 node:crypto，进浏览器产物直接崩；类型在 web/src/api.ts 重声明，人设文案抄一份由 labels 漂移测试看守；见 ADR-0003 |
138|| D12 | 直播与翻历史共用同一个纯函数折叠器 | 两套渲染逻辑迟早会漂移，出现「看着一样、结论不同」；事件流只有来源不同，折叠规则必须唯一 |
139|| D13 | 单端口托管已构建前端，npm run preview = 构建 + 起服 | 评审只会跑一次；两个端口的演示多一步、多一个失败点；见 ADR-0003 |
```

```text
45| * 这样命中后还能把区间还原回原文坐标（前端要高亮原文）。
46| */
47|export function normalizeWithMap(s: string): { norm: string; map: number[] } {
48|  const chars: string[] = [];
49|  const map: number[] = [];
50|  let prevWasSpace = false;
51|  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
52|    const ch = s.charAt(i);
53|    if (/\s/.test(ch)) {
54|      if (!prevWasSpace && chars.length > 0) { chars.push(' '); map.push(i); prevWasSpace = true; }
55|      continue;
56|    }
57|    prevWasSpace = false;
58|    const mapped = (PUNCT_MAP[ch] ?? ch).toLowerCase();
59|    for (let k = 0; k < mapped.length; k++) { chars.push(mapped.charAt(k)); map.push(i); }
60|  }
61|  return { norm: chars.join(''), map };
```

Design of md2html:
- Input: markdown string, options {title, css}.
- Output: HTML string.

Block parsing line-based:
- fenced code: line starting with fence (3+ backticks or tildes) → collect until closing fence; optional lang.
- heading: /^(#{1,6})\s+(.*)$/
- hr: /^(-{3,}|\*{3,})$/
- blockquote: lines starting '> '
- table: line with '|' and next line matching /^\|?[\s:|-]+\|?$/ → parse rows
- lists: /^(\s*)([-*+]|\d+\.)\s+/ with nesting by indent
- paragraph: accumulate until blank

Inline: escape HTML first, then code spans (`...`), bold **, italic *, links [text](url), strikethrough ~~.

Careful with escaping order: escape & < > first, then apply inline code with placeholders to avoid formatting inside code.

Let me write it.

For page counting, simpler: count occurrences of `/Type /Page` excluding `/Pages`. In compressed PDFs the objects may be in object streams — but Edge output we saw gave Count 2. Let's reuse that: regex /\/Count\s+(\d+)/ first match is usually the page tree count. Risky but our test worked. Better: parse with a small heuristic: find all `/Type\s*/Page[^s]` matches; count. For t2.pdf let's verify both.

md2html.mjs content plan:

```js
/**
 * 极简 Markdown → HTML 渲染器（零依赖）。
 * 只实现技术文档真正用到的语法：标题、段落、围栏代码、行内代码、粗斜体、
 * 链接、表格、有序/无序列表（含缩进）、引用块、分隔线。
 * 不追求 CommonMark 兼容——够用、可读、可测即可。
 */

const BT = String.fromCharCode(96);

export function escapeHtml(s) {...}

function inline(text) {
```
